# 00 — Synthesis of the JevCode TUI research (files 01–17 and 19)

Written 2026-09-20 by the synthesizing editor after reading every file under `docs/research/tui/` in full
(7,763 lines for 01–12; updated the same day after reading the gap-fill files 13–17 and the re-verification
file 19 in full, 3,734 more lines). This file adds no new facts: every factual line cites the research file and
section it came from as `(NN §x.y)`, where `NN` is the file prefix (`01` = `01-opencode.md` … `12` =
`12-accessibility-robustness-testing.md`, `13` = `13-first-run-onboarding-without-keys-in-tui.md`, `14` =
`14-cost-guardrails-for-an-interactive-multi.md`, `15` = `15-git-state-awareness-diff-format-and-undo.md`,
`16` = `16-secrets-typed-or-pasted-into-the-compose.md`, `17` = `17-error-provider-failure-and-crash-ux-plus.md`,
`19` = `19-reverification.md`; there is no file 18) and `§x.y` is that file's section number. Unnumbered closing
sections are cited as `(NN ADOPT)`, `(NN REJECT)`, `(NN OPEN)`, `(NN UNVERIFIED)`; 19's verdict rows are cited as
`(19 §n)` and its adopt/reject rows as `(19 V<n>)` / `(19 X<n>)`. Where a file's digest and the file disagreed,
the file's number is used (listed in §5.9). All fetch dates in the sources are 2026-09-20 unless a section says
otherwise.

**Corrections applied from 19.** Every sentence that 19 marked REFUTED, PARTIALLY REFUTED or "wording defect"
has been rewritten in place and tagged `[corrected per 19 §n]`: the "all twelve files" overstatement (19 §1),
the unattributed Claude Code renderer claim (19 §2), the owner of `getWindowSize() = [0,0]` (19 §3), the missing
Ctrl-C matrix (19 §4), the inconsistent height caps (19 §5), `"private": true`/LICENSE in the publish plan
(19 §6), the "future work" secret filter that already exists in `src/core/redact.ts` (19 §7), OSC 52 in "VS Code
1.93" (19 §8), the VS Code 1.109 kitty default (19 §9), the npm-version gap for trusted publishing (19 §10), the
tmux 3.7 conflation (19 §11) and the notification-timer semantics (19 §12).

Constraints every file honoured: Node 22.23.2, TypeScript strict, ESM, one npm package, runtime deps only
`ink@7.1.1` + `react@19.3.0`, esbuild single bundle, first frame < 300 ms with zero network, zero terminal
clears after the first frame, rendering never blocks the loop, keys never in logs (01 preamble; 05 preamble;
06 preamble; 07 preamble; 08 preamble; 09 preamble).

Contents: §1 executive summary · §2 ADOPT (§2.1–2.10 from files 01–12; §2.11–2.16 from files 13–17 and 19) ·
§3 REJECT · §4 conflicts and resolutions · §5 measured numbers (§5.10 from files 13–17 and 19) · §6 terminal
support matrix · §7 UNVERIFIED items and open questions · §8 coverage after files 13–17 · §9 Ready for design
(the 15 decisions the design document must make).

---

## 1. Executive summary (one page)

**Shape.** The best interactive TUI for JevCode under these constraints is a *scrollback-native chat*: every
committed item is appended to Ink's `<Static>` and lives in the terminal's own scrollback, while one
fixed-height dynamic region ≤ `rows − 2` holds, top to bottom, the live tail (≤ 2 rows), a tabbed 12-row Jev
pane (decisions / plan / timeline / synth), the review box when a confirm is pending, the queued-steer preview
(≤ 2–3 rows), the composer (1–6 rows, internal scroll) and a one-row status line (02 §9; 05 §15 A1; 06 ADOPT;
07 §2.4; 08 §9, §12 A7; 10 §15.3; 11 §5; 12 §4). The `rows − 2` budget is the whole game: inside it a
keystroke costs 893 B; four rows over it costs 29,765 B plus one `ESC[2J` replay of the entire transcript
per keystroke (08 §11). The figures in that shape are per-component *maxima*, never simultaneous: their naive
sum is 40–41 rows against a 22-row budget at rows 24, so the design must fix one cap set and a yield order
(composer 1–6, 8 only at rows ≥ 40; queue ≤ 2; pane ≤ 12; review = header 8 + preview ≤ 8; yield pane → live →
preview → queue → composer-to-1) — see A109 and §4 C47 `[corrected per 19 §5]`. By its rendering engineer's own
account (a first-person HN comment of 2026-01-21 quoted in 02 §2.2) Claude Code dropped `<Static>` and rewrote
its renderer from scratch, then paid with upstream terminal patches and an alternate-screen mode (02 §2.2–2.3);
whether the shipped binary still wraps upstream Ink is UNVERIFIED (U2; 19 §2) `[corrected per 19 §2]`. Codex
needed a ratatui fork and four follow-up fixes for its DECSTBM inline viewport for a saving of ~180 B per
committed line (04 §3, §11); Qwen's alt-screen virtual viewport tears in web terminals (05 §4). Alternate screen
is rejected by every file that discusses it (01–08 and 12; 09–11 are silent) and mouse capture by every file that
discusses it (01, 02, 04, 05, 07); files 13–17 never mention either (19 §1 grep table) `[corrected per 19 §1]`.

**Ink 7.1.1 already supplies every terminal primitive the reference tools built by hand** — synchronized
output on every frame, bracketed paste (`usePaste`), the real cursor (`useCursor`), `suspendTerminal` for
`$EDITOR`/`$PAGER`, `useWindowSize`, kitty CSI-u parsing — but no text buffer, no scrolling and no multi-line
input, and the two npm inputs are single-line and 28 months stale (03 §2.4; 05 §13.2; 07 §0; 08 §0, §7).
The composer is therefore hand-rolled: a pure reducer over a grapheme-indexed buffer (`Intl.Segmenter`, 251
ns/grapheme), a ~60-line width function replicating `string-width` 8.2.2's rules, `useCursor` placed from
summed widths so IME lands in the composer, `usePaste` with a `[Pasted #n, k lines]` placeholder above 3
lines / 800 chars, and an input filter that drops the control bytes and CSI bodies Ink leaks as text
(`[I`, `[24;80R`, `[27;2;13~`, `[?0u`) (03 §20 A3–A4; 07 §1.2; 08 §2.3, §8; 12 §7).

**Keys.** Readline/emacs bindings exactly as bash, Node readline, helix, bubbles, fish and Claude Code agree
(06 §4). Enter submits; Ctrl+J, `\`+Enter and Alt+Enter insert a newline in every terminal; Shift/Ctrl+Enter
only when a keyboard protocol delivers `CSI 13;2u` (02 §3.1; 06 §0 M1; 07 §1.4; 08 §2.3). `/` at column 0
opens the palette (never Ctrl+P or Ctrl+K, which are readline history/kill-line), `@` opens file mention
over the engine's candidate list, `?` on an empty composer opens help (06 §2–3, §17). Ink's kitty `auto`
mode is not usable as shipped — it writes `CSI ? u` before the first frame, waits a fixed 200 ms with no DA1
sentinel, and the reply leaks into `useInput` as `"[?0u"` even on success (07 §1.2) — so v1 ships with it
disabled and the universal newline keys; an owned `CSI ? u` + `CSI c` handshake with flag 1 only follows once
the pty suite exists (07 §1.2, §10; 12 REJECT). Esc is context-sensitive: it declines a pending review (11
§4j), otherwise pauses a live run at the next step boundary (`human_pause`, 10 §15.4); Esc Esc clears a
draft to history (02 §3.8) or aborts a live run (10 §15.4); a 30 ms re-buffer sits above Ink's fixed 20 ms
flush because `ESC` + `x` 25 ms apart is already delivered as Esc then x (07 §1.5). Ctrl-C keeps DESIGN §11's
clear → `human_abort` → exit semantics (07 §1.6; 10 §15.4); Ctrl-D on an empty composer exits after a confirm
hint (02 §3.8; 06 §1).

**Jev-native review.** None of opencode, Claude Code, Codex or Gemini shows *why* an action was flagged; all
four offer some remembered "always" (11 §3.5). JevCode does the opposite: the review box leads with the four
risk dimensions as per-dimension gauges (level, 10-cell bar with band-coloured track, risk, bound, confidence,
level text), keys are `y` / `n` / `Esc` / `d` (decline with a redacted note fed back as the reason) / `e`
(expand preview) / `w`+digit (`/why`), the keys line moves to row 2 so it survives truncation at rows 12, the
prompt is deferred until the composer has been idle ~1 s with buffered input drained, and there is no remembered
approval, no Enter default and no approve-on-timeout (04 §8, §12 A9; 11 §4b, §4j, §6 A1–A3). Decision rows get
eighth-block probability bars and two-decimal `p`/`c`; `/why` and `/calibration` are appended `<Static>`
blocks computed from `decisions.jsonl` with zero engine change (11 §4a, §4h, §4i).

**Sessions and steering.** A session is an ordered list of runs; a follow-up is a *new* run seeded with the
parent's `plan.done`/`remaining`, last 4 window entries, `createdThisRun`, `lastTestRun` and a
`HarnessProblem { kind: 'human' }`; mid-run steering is queued in `CheckpointState.pendingDirectives` and
consumed at step start after `checkBudgets()` (10 §15.1–15.3). `~/.jevcode/sessions/index.jsonl` is the
picker's only list source: 0.13 ms to read versus 47 ms to scan every `run.json`+`state.json` and 14.9 ms to
parse one large `steps.jsonl` (10 §14, §15.6). `/undo` restores per-step pre-images and, for tracked files a
`run` step changed while they were clean, `git restore --source=HEAD --worktree -- <path>` — never
`git checkout -- <path>`, which restores from the *index* and left a staged edit in place when probed (15 §3.2,
§6.4; supersedes 10 §15.7's wording, see §4 C40); every restore is preceded by a sha256 comparison against the
step's recorded post-image so user or later-step edits are never overwritten silently (15 §6.3–6.4). Run-step
pre-images are a copy of the currently dirty/untracked/touched set, not `git stash create` (44.8–84.4 ms p50 and
12 loose objects per 3 files, measured 15 §4); whole-tree shadow git per step is rejected against the 50 ms
harness budget (10 §15.7, §17; 15 §9). SQLite is out: `node:sqlite` prints an ExperimentalWarning on 22.23.2
(10 §17).

**Terminal hygiene.** ANSI-16 named colours only, a marker or word beside every colour, no backgrounds (06
§13; 11 §3.3; 12 §3.2). `NO_COLOR` must be mapped to `FORCE_COLOR=0` before the bundle loads because chalk
5.6.2 never reads it (06 §0; 07 §2.10; 11 §3.3). No OSC 11 background query through Ink — its parser shreds
an OSC reply into three key events (07 §1.2) — so themes are `--theme dark|light|daltonized|ansi` with no
auto-detect (06 §14; 12 §3.3). One idempotent exit string in every exit path; `stdin/stdout/stderr` error
listeners before SIGHUP logic (without them the HUP handler never ran); SIGTSTP/SIGCONT handled explicitly
(nothing re-enables raw mode on CONT); 50 ms resize debounce (30 SIGWINCH → 30 resize events) (07 §3).
`--screen-reader` uses Ink's SR mode with `aria-hidden` spinner and a status line that changes only on stage
transitions; reduced motion keeps a 1 Hz functional tick (12 §1.5, §2).

**Packaging and startup.** One npm package with zero install-time dependencies, explicit `files`, a minified
bundle (2.17 → 1.08 MB), static shell completions, `jevcode upgrade` delegating to the detected package
manager, an update notifier that is off by default and runs only after a run ends, a Homebrew tap in the
Node-formula shape, trusted publishing (09 §11–12). Snapshots fail and SEA gives parity, not speed (09
§10.6–10.7). The first frame is 80–90 ms today, of which Ink/React/yoga evaluation is ~33 ms (09 §10.1,
§10.5), so a composer frame has ~200 ms of headroom but must itself be covered by the gate (10 §18 Q8).

**Testing.** Pure units → `ink-testing-library` with a stdout that has `rows` (else 2.34 ms per layout via
`/dev/tty`) → normalised `renderToString` snapshots of the dynamic region only → an `expect`-driven pty project
on macOS with `CI` unset → perf gates extended to `ESC[3J`, `ESC c`, `ESC[?1049h` and frame counts via
`ESC[?2026h` (08 §1.3; 12 §12).

**Gap-fill files 13–17 (written after the first synthesis; each closes a §8 gap).** *Onboarding (13):* the
missing-key failure lands *after* the first frame because `config.generator()`/`config.decider()` validate lazily
(13 §1.1), so a non-throwing `config.missingSecrets(mode)` probe drives a ≤ 4-row wizard (provider → masked
generator key → masked Jev key with "Enter = reuse the OpenRouter key" → atomic `0600` write to
`${XDG_CONFIG_HOME:-~/.config}/jevcode/config.json` → optional priced verification on explicit `y` → per-git-root
trust gate → one sandbox line → composer); the masked field is `<Text>` bullets + `useCursor` + `usePaste` + the one
`useInput`, measured at zero key bytes on the pty in five scenarios and 2.4–2.9 ms Enter-to-saved (13 §4.1,
§5.2–5.7); today's `JEVCODE_TRACE` line leaked a 108-char key whole and as 108 one-char lines (13 §4.1 C/D).
*Cost (14):* session spend is unbounded by construction under A52; the fix is a parent `SpendMeter` per session
with each run as `child(min(runCap, remaining))`, default 5 × run cap ($10.00), `budget:warn` at 50/80/95 % as a
`<Static>` item + toast + meter word, a follow-up confirm box only when `0 < remaining < runCap`, and unknown
pricing failing *closed* (today an unlisted Anthropic model shows `cost gen $0.000` forever because the warning
is computed and never printed, 14 §1.3, §4.1–4.5). *Git (15):* one `status --porcelain=v2 --branch -uall -z`
(13.9 ms on 5,041 files) plus one combined `rev-parse` replace four serial spawns; a notice-only run-start banner;
a status-line git zone fed by `fs.watch(<gitDir>)` filtered on `HEAD` (the file watcher dies after git's first
lock+rename); per-step post-image hashes; a verify-before-write `/undo`; an inline `--numstat` stat block and
`/diff --full` through `$PAGER` via `suspendTerminal`; and a seatbelt profile fix for linked worktrees, where the
harness's own `git restore` fails today with `index.lock: Operation not permitted` (15 §4–6). *Secrets (16):* the
"heuristic secret filter" P19 called future work already exists (`src/core/redact.ts` `FORMAT_PATTERNS`, 16 §1.1;
19 §7); the composer adds five sinks (provider request — raw by design —, `history.jsonl`, `ui.json` draft,
clipboard, `--json`), closed by a `detectSecrets` pre-submit row (`Send anyway? y/N`, only `y` sends), one
`addSecret` on `y` that masks every downstream artefact, paste bodies kept only in a `useRef`, an `@` denylist
reusing `isSecretPath()`, and history **on by default** but written only through `redact` — 0 false positives on
~50 MB of real agent text for fourteen families, ~9 FP/MB for gitleaks' `generic-api-key` (16 §3.2, §4). *Errors
(17):* neither retry loop is observable (a 429 with `Retry-After: 12` shows `step 0/1 ⠋ starting` for 24 s), Ink's
own ErrorOverview is 42 rows and clears screen *and* scrollback (`ESC[2J ESC[3J`), ENOSPC exits 0 with 202
identical lines and no `state.json`, a 401 burns three steps in 71 ms as exit 5, and `fatalExit` prints while raw
mode is still on (17 §1); the design adds `retry` events + a 1 Hz row, a `PaneBoundary` per pane, a re-ordered
`fatalExit` with a stderr epilogue via `fs.writeSync`, a disk-error pause with exit 3, 401 → exit 2 + onboarding
pane, a per-run `jevcode.log`, and `jevcode report` (17 §4).

**Re-verification (19).** Twelve summary claims were re-checked against primary sources and today's measurements:
seven hold as stated or with a wording fix, three are refuted (secret filter, OSC 52 "1.93", kitty "1.109 default
true") and two partially (`"private": true`, tmux 3.7 conflation); the corrections are applied throughout and
listed in the preamble. Two new design rules follow from it: never read `process.stdout.columns/rows` for layout
(a 0×0 pty gives 0 while Ink renders 80×24, 19 §3) and never detect a terminal feature by version string (VS Code's
kitty default was `false` behind an experiment at 1.109 and `true` only from 1.110, 19 §9).

## 2. Consolidated ADOPT

Each row names the source file and section that supports it; where several files agree, all are listed.

### 2.1 Composer / input

| # | What | Why | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A1 | Hand-rolled `TextBuffer` + `layout` in `src/tui/composer/*`: logical lines, UTF-16 cursor always on a grapheme boundary, moved by grapheme via `Intl.Segmenter` (word granularity for Alt+b/f, Ctrl+W); pure reducer, 100-snapshot undo/redo, `set_text(pushToUndo)`; unit-tested without Ink | `ink-text-input`/`@inkjs/ui` are single-line, string-index, chalk-dependent and unreleased since May 2024; Segmenter costs 0.37 ms per 3,240 chars and 251 ns/grapheme | 08 §7, §8, §12 A1; 03 §3, §20 A3–A4; 12 §7, ADOPT |
| A2 | Minimal `cellWidth` replicating `string-width` 8.2.2 (zero-width classes via `\p{…}` `v`-flag, `\p{RGI_Emoji}` → 2, keycap/ZWJ → 2, Hangul L+V(+T) → 2, EAW Wide/Fullwidth table of 125 + 3 ranges generated at build from `get-east-asian-width`, ambiguous = narrow); fixture test against `string-width` | Ink measures with `string-width`, so composer `x` must match its rules; importing it directly breaks the one-package rule | 08 §8, §12 A1; 12 §7, ADOPT; 07 §4 (parity rule) |
| A3 | Real cursor via `useCursor().setCursorPosition` computed from summed grapheme widths during render; no inverse-block fake cursor; DECSCUSR steady bar on start | IME preedit anchors to the real cursor; cursor-only frames are a few bytes | 08 §3, §12 A2; 05 §15 A6; 07 §2.8, §10; 12 §7 |
| A4 | `usePaste`; normalise `\r\n` then `\r` → `\n`; strip C0/ESC except `\n\t`; one undo step; collapse pastes > 3 lines or > 800 chars into `[Pasted #n, k lines]` kept in memory (never logs) and expanded at submit; treat a multi-char `useInput` chunk as paste-like when bracketed paste is absent (no timers). **Lifecycle (16):** the payload lives in a `useRef<Map<n, PasteBlob>>` (never React state, reducer or `EngineEvent`); hard cap 1 MiB per paste; `ui.json` stores `{ n, lines, bytes, sha256 }` chips and a pre-redacted draft only, written at checkpoint boundaries, never per keystroke; on `--resume` an unrestored chip cancels submission with `remove [Pasted #1] or paste again`; history stores the label `[Pasted #n, k lines, sha256:xxxxxxxx]` | Ink delivers split markers and 20 KB pastes as one event (measured); placeholder keeps the composer in budget; pastes never submit; Claude Code keeps paste bodies under `~/.claude/paste-cache/` for 30 days — the leak class 16 closes | 07 §1.1, §10; 08 §2.4, §12 A3; 02 §3.3, ADOPT; 04 §5, §12 A6 (normalisation order); 16 §2.3, §2.7, §4.3, ADOPT A5; thresholds differ per file — see §4 C10 |
| A5 | Filter `input` before insertion: drop when `key.ctrl/meta/super/hyper`, drop `eventType` release/repeat, drop code units < 0x20 except `\t` and the newline keys, drop `0x7F`, drop CSI-body leak-through (`[I`, `[O`, `[24;80R`, `[27;2;13~`, `[?0u`, `[?62;22c`, `[<64;10;5M`) | Probes show all of these arrive in `useInput` as text | 08 §2.3, §12 A5; 07 §1.2 item 6, §2.2, §2.3; 06 §0 M1 |
| A6 | Newline keys: Ctrl+J (`input === '\n'`, name `enter`, no `key.return`), `\`+Enter, Alt+Enter (`return && meta`); Shift/Ctrl+Enter when a protocol delivers `return && shift/ctrl`; detect and swallow xterm `CSI 27;mod;13~` | Only universally available paths; legacy Shift+Enter is byte-identical to Enter; Ink does not parse the xterm form | 07 §1.4, §10; 08 §2.3, §12 A4, §13 R3; 06 §0 M1, §7, ADOPT; 02 §3.1, ADOPT; 05 §15 A3 |
| A7 | Up/Down move within multi-line text; history only from the first/last visual row (or cursor 0 / untouched buffer); Ctrl+P/N always history; Ctrl+R reverse-incremental search with live preview, Esc restores the draft, Tab/Esc accept, Enter submits | opencode/Claude Code/Codex/Gemini rule; disambiguates arrows in a multi-line editor | 01 §2.1, ADOPT; 02 §3.2, §3.6, ADOPT; 03 §4; 04 §5, §12 A4; 06 §5, §17.2 |
| A8 | Readline set: C-a/e (Home/End), C-b/f, M-b/f + C-←/→, C-k/C-u, C-w/M-Backspace, M-d/M-Delete/C-Delete, C-y/M-y kill ring (single-entry survives submit at minimum), C-t, C-_ (`0x1f`) undo, C-l = erase-lines repaint (never `ESC[2J`) | Every reference input agrees; Ctrl+L must not clear scrollback | 06 §4, §17.2, ADOPT; 02 §3.2; 04 §5, §12 A4; 07 §1.6 |
| A9 | Submit re-entrancy guard; typing `exit`/`quit`/`:q` exits; rotating placeholder examples | Double Enter otherwise sends a phantom empty prompt | 01 §2.1, ADOPT |
| A10 | Composer 1–6 rows (8 only when `rows ≥ 40`) inside `rows − 2` with `overflow="hidden"` viewport, `scrollTop`, `↑N`/`↓N` gutter markers; decisions pane shrinks first; composer-only layout below 8 rows; status-only below 3; `MIN 40×8` notice with transcript still flowing. The cap is one of the single cap set fixed in A109 (composer 1–6/8, queue ≤ 2, pane ≤ 12, review 8 + ≤ 8) with yield order pane → live → preview → queue → composer-to-1 `[corrected per 19 §5: the summary previously carried three composer caps — 1–6 here, ≤ 8 in A107, "1 growing to 5–6" in Q8]` | 893 B vs 29,765 B + `ESC[2J` per keystroke; measured 0 clears at 5×20 and 0×0; the §1 shape summed to 40–41 rows on a 22-row budget until one cap set was fixed | 08 §8, §11, §12 A7; 07 §3.7, §10; 12 §4, ADOPT; 05 §15 A1; 11 §5; 19 §5, V5 |
| A11 | Ctrl+G external editor (`$VISUAL` ‖ `$EDITOR`, temp file, one undo snapshot) via `suspendTerminal(async () => spawn(..., {stdio:'inherit'}))`; queue transcript items while suspended and flush after `resume()` | `<Static>` items committed during suspension are silently dropped (real-pty probe); child output stays in scrollback | 08 §5, §12 A8–A9; 03 §3, §20 A6; 01 ADOPT (`/editor`); 04 §5 |
| A12 | Change `src/tui/App.tsx:126` `JEVCODE_TRACE` line to log key *classes* only (`kind=<return\|backspace\|ctrl\|escape\|text> len=<n> masked=<bool>`; `usePaste` logs `len` only), unconditionally — not only inside a masked field; fold `JEVCODE_TRACE` into the `trace` level of the per-run log (A168) | Hard constraint "keys never appear in logs"; **measured**: a copy of today's line wrote a 108-char key whole for a non-bracketed paste and as 108 consecutive one-char lines that reconstruct it when typed; bracketed pastes were missed only because they go to `usePaste` | 04 §12 A16; 08 §0 item 12, §12 A15; 11 §4j, §6 A14; 12 §0, ADOPT; 13 §4.1 C/D, §5.3, ADOPT; 16 §1.3, §4.8, ADOPT A10; 17 §0.4, §4.10 |
| A13 | No `useFocus` anywhere in the composer tree (or `disableFocus()`); Tab = completion, Esc = context action | With any focusable registered, Tab/Shift+Tab/Esc are also consumed by Ink's focus manager (probe) | 08 §2.6, §12 A13 |
| A14 | Keep `render({ exitOnCtrlC: false, patchConsole: false })`; one Ctrl-C handler `key.ctrl && input === 'c'` | Works for `\x03` and kitty `CSI 99;5u`; App-level check only sees `\x03` | 08 §2.3, §6, §12 A12; 03 §1, §20 A1 |
| A15 | Replace the hand-rolled `useTerminalSize` with Ink `useWindowSize`; every test fake stdout gets `rows` | Same semantics; avoids `terminal-size` opening `/dev/tty` (2.34 ms per layout) | 08 §1.3, §12 A10; 12 §12.2 |

### 2.2 Keymap

| # | What | Why | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A16 | One typed command registry `{ name, title, category, slashName, slashAliases, keys, hidden, availableDuringTask, run }` feeding the dispatcher, the `/` palette ("Suggested" first, binding shown per row), the `?` overlay, the status-line ShortHelp, `jevcode keys` and a generated `docs/KEYS.md` with a sync test | Single source of truth; names and docs cannot drift (opencode, lazygit, bubbles, Textual, Gemini all do this) | 01 §2.4, §2.6, §8 item 1, ADOPT; 06 §2, ADOPT; 03 §2.3, §20 A5; 04 §9, §12 A7 |
| A17 | Explicit key contexts — Global, Composer, Review (takes precedence), Palette/Help/Picker (modal) — resolved by one matcher that reads buffer/engine state, never by subscription order | Gemini's Ctrl+C clear-vs-quit depends on React effect ordering | 06 §17; 03 §17, §21 R14 |
| A18 | Ctrl-C: composer non-empty → clear (draft to history); empty + live run → `human_abort` (checkpoint; exit 130 in one-shot mode, `run:end` item and composer reopens in session mode); empty + idle in session mode → status-line hint, second press exits 0; second press while `aborting` is set → `process.exit` immediately (DESIGN §11 invariant); window length to decide (1 s Codex, 1.5 s, 3 s Gemini). | A19 | Ctrl-D: empty composer → hint, exit on second press within 800 ms (confirm while a run is live); non-empty → delete-forward | readline EOF; Claude Code 800 ms | 02 §3.8, ADOPT; 06 §1, §17.1; 07 §1.6, §12 Q8; 10 §15.9 |

| A70 | Update notifier **off by default**; when enabled, a post-run detached `unref()`'d `jevcode upgrade --check --write-cache` writes `$XDG_CACHE_HOME/jevcode/update-check.json`, read after the first frame on the next run; suppressed under `CI`/`CONTINUOUS_INTEGRATION`, `NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`/`JEVCODE_NO_UPDATE_CHECK`, non-TTY/`--plain`, npx, < 24 h | Preserves zero network before the first frame; update-notifier's proven model with Node built-ins | 09 §4, §11.5, §12; 01 ADOPT (notify-only after the first frame); 02 REJECT (launch network) |

| A72 | Trusted publishing (OIDC, `id-token: write`) from GitHub Actions on tags; GitHub Release with the tarball + `SHA256SUMS`; dist-tags `latest`/`next`; `prepare` guard against direct publish; per-version CHANGELOG; `--version` smoke test in CI. **The publish job must run `npm install -g npm@11` (npm 11.5.1's `engines` is `^20.17.0 \|\| >=22.9.0`) or run on Node ≥ 24.5.0, and assert `npm --version ≥ 11.5.1` before `npm publish`; `.nvmrc` stays 22.23.2 for runtime/tests** `[addendum per 19 §10: no Node 22.x release bundles npm 11 — 22.22.3 → 10.9.8 and this machine's 22.23.2 reports 10.9.8; the first Node to bundle 11.5.1 is 24.5.0 (2025-07-31)]` | Provenance is automatic under trusted publishing (docs: "requires npm CLI version 11.5.1 or later and Node version 22.14.0 or higher"; public repo and public package only); Codex's registry entry shows SLSA v1 | 09 §9, §11.7, §12; 02 §1.1, ADOPT; 01 §5, ADOPT; 19 §10, V10, X5 |
| A73 | Honour `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` for `config.json`, use `XDG_CACHE_HOME` for caches, keep `~/.jevcode/runs` (`JEVCODE_HOME`) for run state | XDG defaults; the seatbelt profile and resume format key on `~/.jevcode` | 09 §7, §11.6, §12 |

| A75 | `jevcode doctor` (read-only, offline): version, bundle path, Node version, install method, paths, sandbox level, compile-cache status, completion status, negotiated terminal capabilities (`--terminal`), config sources, `Fix:` lines | `claude doctor` is the model; the terminal matrix has too many `?` cells without a repeatable probe | 02 §1.5, ADOPT; 09 §11.5, §12; 07 §8, §10 |

### 2.8 Terminal protocols and edge cases

| # | What | Why | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A77 | `kittyKeyboard: { mode: 'disabled' }` in v1; later an owned handshake: write `CSI ? u` + `CSI c` together after the first frame, wait ≥ 1 s, consume `/^\[\?[\d;]*[uc]$/` replies in `useInput`, then push `CSI > 1 u` (flag 1 only); pop `CSI < u` exactly once in every exit path; `JEVCODE_TUI_DISABLE_KEYBOARD_ENHANCEMENT` kill switch | Ink auto mode writes the query before the first frame, times out at 200 ms with no DA1 sentinel, and leaks the reply into `useInput` as `"[?0u"` (measured); flag 1 keeps Enter/Tab/Backspace legacy so `reset` stays typeable after a crash | 07 §1.2, §10, §11; 04 §12 A5 (kill switch); 12 §7, REJECT; see §4 C1 |
| A78 | Inside tmux (`TMUX` set) send `CSI > 4 ; 2 m`, decode both `CSI 27;m;k~` and `CSI k;m u`, reset `CSI > 4 m` on exit | tmux has no kitty protocol; `extended-keys` is its modifier path and Claude Code documents the user-side config | 07 §1.3, §10 |
| A79 | Send only CSI-shaped queries (kitty, DA1, `CSI ? 2026 $ p`) and filter their single-string replies; no OSC 11 / XTVERSION unless a pre-Ink stdin demux exists; never any query before the first frame | Ink's parser has no OSC/DCS state: an OSC 11 reply arrived as three key events (measured) | 07 §1.2, §2.10, §10; 06 §14; 12 §3.3, REJECT |
| A80 | One idempotent exit string (`CSI < u` if pushed · `CSI > 4 m` if set · `?1004l` · `?2004l` · `?2026l` · `CSI 0 SP q` · `?25h` · `SGR 0` · `\r\n` if mid-line) then `setRawMode(false)` synchronously in the `'exit'` handler; written in `fatal()`, SIGTSTP, SIGHUP and unmount; never `ESC c` or `ESC[2J` | signal-exit misses SIGKILL/hangs; Node's default terminal reset is removed once SIGINT/SIGTERM listeners exist; ESU releases a terminal frozen mid-BSU | 07 §2.1, §3.1, §3.5, §10; 12 §10, ADOPT, REJECT; 04 §4, §12 A14; 03 §2.1, §20 A20 |
| A81 | Install `stdin`/`stdout`/`stderr` `'error'` listeners (EIO/EPIPE) before any SIGHUP logic; on stdin `'end'` or SIGHUP write the checkpoint, never write to the terminal, exit 129 | Without the listeners an EIO write error crashed the process before the SIGHUP handler ran (measured) | 07 §3.4, §10 |
| A82 | Composer requires `stdin.isTTY && stdout.isTTY && !CI && TERM !== 'dumb'`; otherwise plain renderer, no queries, no 2004/1004; use `Boolean(isTTY)` | `isTTY` is `undefined` on a pipe; Ink is non-interactive under `CI` | 07 §3.6; 09 §4; 12 §12.8 |
| A83 | `bin/jevcode.js` env shim: `NO_COLOR` non-empty and `FORCE_COLOR` unset → `FORCE_COLOR=0`; `TERM=dumb` or `CI` → plain; guard `getColorDepth` with `typeof`; 16 named colours inside tmux; one `colorEnabled()` (flag > `FORCE_COLOR` > `NO_COLOR` > `TERM=dumb` > `hasColors?.(16)`); add `--no-color` to `FLAGS`; a first-position side-effect import so tests see the same behaviour | chalk 5.6.2 has no `NO_COLOR` check; Node's depth table rates `xterm-ghostty` as 16 colours and any `TMUX` as truecolor; `getColorDepth` does not exist on pipes | 06 §0 M3, §13, ADOPT; 07 §2.10, §10, §12 Q10; 11 §3.3, §6 A9; 12 §3.1, ADOPT |
| A84 | Keep `<Static>` scrollback; no alternate screen; no mouse reporting (1000/1002/1003/1006); filter SGR mouse noise `/^\[<\d+;\d+;\d+[Mm]$/` | Wheel must keep scrolling native history; drag-selection needs Shift when a program grabs the mouse; Ink has no mouse decoder | 07 §2.3–2.4, §10; 01 REJECT; 02 REJECT; 04 §13 R8; 05 §16 R2 |
| A85 | Notifications opt-in (default off; on in screen-reader mode): BEL; OSC 9 for iTerm2/Ghostty/WezTerm/foot, OSC 99 for kitty; DCS tmux passthrough; never start an OSC 9 payload with `<digit>;`; review notification = an **idle gate with debounce**: the timer starts when the *deferred* review box appears (A41), every keystroke restarts it, it fires at ~6 s; run-end notification starts at `run:end`, is cancelled by any keystroke and fires at ~60 s; also at the 95 % budget threshold and `budget:stop` when unfocused (14); payload is a redacted one-liner `[clarified per 19 §12: Claude Code's ~6 s is "once you haven't typed for about six seconds … each keystroke defers it", a fixed delay only in SDK-hosted sessions]` | Matches the terminals' docs and Claude Code's channel set/timing (docs:hooks `permission_prompt`/`idle_prompt`); foot/Ghostty treat `9;<n>;` as ConEmu progress | 07 §2.7, §10; 02 §5.2, §7.3, ADOPT; 05 §15 A13; 12 §1.5; 14 §4.2; 19 §12, V12, X10 |
| A86 | `/copy [last\|proposal\|diff\|draft]`: payload = `config.redact(sanitizeStream(x))` (the exact function, idempotent, 0.08 ms per 64 KB), 64 KiB cap, native tool first (`pbcopy`, `wl-copy`, `xclip -selection clipboard`, `xsel --clipboard --input`), OSC 52 **write** only behind `--osc52`/`ui.osc52` with the tmux DCS wrapper (doubled ESC); `/copy draft` is the only unredacted source and reports `copied with 1 secret masked`; never OSC 52 read (`?`) | Terminal.app drops OSC 52; iTerm2 needs a preference; reads prompt or are denied; transcript items are already redacted at `emit`, the draft is not | 12 §9, ADOPT; 07 §2.5, §10, §11; 01 ADOPT; 05 §15 A19; 16 §3.1, §4.5, ADOPT A7; see §4 C12 |
| A87 | OSC 8 hyperlinks only for self-resolved `file://` paths on adoption-list terminals; `ESC \` as ST | Ink's `<Text>` passes OSC through; an attacker-controlled URI is a phishing vector; Terminal.app is absent from the adoption list | 07 §2.6, §10 |
| A88 | `sanitizeStream` remains the single choke point (C0 except `\t\n\r`, DEL, C1) for transcript items, live region, pastes and clipboard payloads; also strip bidi controls U+202A–202E and U+2066–2069, normalise U+2028/2029 to `\n`, keep U+200D | Ink's sanitizer keeps SGR *and* OSC, so untrusted text could write the clipboard, spoof links or set titles | 07 §0, §5, §10; 12 §0 |
| A89 | Terminal title opt-in (`--title`), `OSC 2 ; jevcode: <task head> ST` with control/bidi characters stripped, cleared (`OSC 2 ; ST`) on exit | Titles are not reliably restorable across terminals; title text comes from untrusted task/model text | 07 §2.9, §11; 04 §12 A18; see §4 C14 |
| A90 | Unicode gate: `TERM !== 'linux'` && UTF-8 locale on POSIX, `is-unicode-supported` allow-list on Windows; `--ascii` / `JEVCODE_ASCII=1` / auto when `TERM=dumb`; glyph table (`─`→`-`, MiniDot→Line/Ellipsis, `✓ ✗ ! ~ ↩`→`+ x ! ~ <`, `borderStyle="classic"`, `▌░`→`#-`); eighth-block bars over `·`; ASCII `[x] [ ] [?] [!]` ledger; letters for the timeline strip; braille only for the opt-in unicode spinner; never `⚠️` with VS16 | A-class blocks measure 1 cell in `string-width` (already relied on for `─`); braille has documented font gaps; `⚠️` measures 2 | 06 §15, ADOPT; 11 §3.1, §6 A10, §7 |
| A91 | Slow links: lower fps under `SSH_TTY`/`SSH_CONNECTION`, keep the 50 ms coalescer, ≥ 1 s query budgets, never block the first frame on a reply; mosh treated as unsupported for OSC 52/1004/kitty/2026 | Round trips and bandwidth dominate; queries simply get no answer under mosh | 07 §6, §10; 12 §5 |
| A92 | IME: treat any multi-code-point chunk without ESC as text insertion; cursor/deletion by grapheme; do not infer Alt from `å`-style text | Terminals commit IME text as ordinary bytes; Option-as-Meta off sends plain text | 07 §1.7; 08 §8 |
| A93 | Sizes: 0×0 pty → Ink 80×24 fallback (`ink/build/utils.js` `columns \|\| fallbackSize.columns \|\| 80`); composer-only below 8 rows; status-only below 3; `COLUMNS`/`LINES` honoured by Ink's fallback. **Rule:** no JevCode code may read `process.stdout.columns`/`rows` for layout — use Ink's `useWindowSize()`; add a 0×0-pty unit test asserting the rule width is 80 | Measured 0 clears at 5×20 and 0×0; a 0×0 pty is what `expect` and `pty.fork` spawn; re-measured 19 §13: `stdout.columns/rows` 0/0, Node `getWindowSize()` `[0,0]`, `terminal-size` `{0,0}`, Ink `{80,24}` (and `{100,30}` with `COLUMNS=100 LINES=30`) | 07 §3.7, §10; 12 §4, §12.6; 19 §3, §13, V3, X8 |

| # | What | Why | Source |

| A94 | `--screen-reader` > `JEVCODE_SCREEN_READER` (`=0` overrides) > `ui.screenReader`; pass `isScreenReaderEnabled` to `render()`; also honour `INK_SCREEN_READER`; print `[screen reader mode: on via flag\|env\|config]` as the first `<Static>` line; **no** auto-detection of assistive tech | Ink's SR mode is real (unthrottled, `aria-*`, no cursor hide — measured); Gemini/Claude Code expose the same triad; Gemini #7777 shows config-only wiring breaks; Codex's 450 ms probe would eat the first-frame budget | 12 §1.1, §1.2, §1.5, ADOPT, REJECT; 03 §13, §20 A16; 02 §2.5 |
| A95 | In SR mode: `aria-hidden` spinner; `aria-label` status that changes only on stage transitions; announcements as `<Static>` lines with `you:`/`steer:` prefixes; live stream region off; wall time only in committed lines; review as a numbered list + `Enter selection (1-2):`, typed `y`/`n` + Enter, one BEL; ARIA roles `textbox`/`multiline`, `list`/`listitem` | Codex #11823/#44728: in-place rewrites and timer ticks are re-announced; Ink's SR path still erases and rewrites the dynamic region on every change (20 erases for 20 ticks measured) | 12 §1.1, §1.3–1.5, ADOPT; 08 §4, §12 A14; 02 §2.5, ADOPT |
| A96 | `--no-animation` / `JEVCODE_REDUCED_MOTION` / `ui.reducedMotion` (implied by `--screen-reader`): static marker instead of spinner, a 1 Hz *functional* tick for wall time, `LIVE_FLUSH_MS` raised to 250 ms; a unit test greps `src/tui/**` for `setInterval(` outside the spinner module | WCAG 2.2.2; Codex #46111 froze the timer when animations were disabled; Codex PR #20564's source-scan guard | 12 §2, ADOPT; 04 §10, §12 A12; 02 §7.4 |
| A97 | Colour never alone: verdict word or glyph beside every colour; ANSI-16 named colours only; no backgrounds; red↔cyan/blue pairs rather than red↔green; `--theme dark\|light\|daltonized\|ansi` honouring `NO_COLOR`; no `auto`/OSC 11 detection in v1 | WCAG 1.4.1; ~8 % of Caucasian males red–green deficient; Claude Code ships daltonized themes; JevCode's pane is red/yellow/green today | 06 §13, ADOPT; 12 §3.2, ADOPT; 11 §3.3, §6 A9; 02 §7.7, ADOPT; 05 §15 A16 |
| A98 | `--plain` gains screen-reader labels (`you:`, `jev:`, `generator:`, `tool:`, `tool error:`, `error:`, `warning:`, `Review required:`, `Cost:`) and a readline composer (`createInterface`) with the same slash commands; `--screen-reader` on a non-TTY implies `--plain` | Doubles as the log format; `--plain` already has every text twin | 02 §2.5, ADOPT; 12 §11; 06 §16 |
| A99 | No localisation now: one strings module, width-based alignment instead of `padEnd`/`.length`, fixed number/duration formats, truncate by grapheme | Preserves TUI / `--plain` / `transcript.log` line parity and snapshot stability | 12 §8, ADOPT |
| A100 | Minimum 40×8 with a two-line degradation (notice + composer, transcript keeps flowing); below 80 columns hide probabilities but keep verdict words | Gemini `isNarrowWidth < 80`; Codex tests narrow geometries | 12 §4, ADOPT |

### 2.11 Corrections and rules from the re-verification (19)

| # | What | Why | Source |

| A109 | **One cap set** for the dynamic region: composer 1–6 rows (8 only at `rows ≥ 40`), queue ≤ 2, tabbed pane ≤ 12, review = header 8 + preview ≤ 8, wizard ≤ 4 (13), follow-up confirm 5 (14), secret row 1 (16), retry row inside the ≤ 2-row live region (17); explicit yield order pane → live → preview → queue → composer-to-1; worked 24-row example: no review = 1 status + 1 rule + 2 live + 2 queue + 6 composer = 12 → pane 10; review pending = 1 + 1 + 0 live + 8 header + 2 queue + 1 composer = 13 → preview 8 → pane 1 (or preview 7, pane 2); 12- and 40-row examples to be written in the design | The §1 shape summed to 40–41 rows with a review pending and 24–25 without, against a 22-row budget; any frame over `rows − 2` costs 29,765 B + `ESC[2J` per keystroke | 19 §5, V5, X7; 08 §11, §12 A7; 04 §14 Q4; 10 §15.3; 11 §5; 05 §17 Q1 |
| A110 | Write the Ctrl-C matrix into the design (19 §4's table as the starting proposal: idle-empty session → hint then exit 0; idle-text → clear draft; live-empty one-shot → `shutdown('human_abort')` exit 130; live-empty session → `engine.abort` + `run:end` item, no exit; live-text → clear, never abort; review pending → decline *and* abort) and keep DESIGN §11's "second Ctrl-C while `aborting` → `process.exit`" invariant | Four incompatible readings coexisted (A18, A54/10 §15.4, 06 §17.1, DESIGN §11 + `App.tsx:128-131`); no file enumerated the state × mode matrix | 19 §4, V4, OPEN 1; 17 §4.9 |
| A111 | Detect terminal features at runtime (DA1 + `CSI ? u` reply, `CSI ? 2026 $ p`), never by version string; matrix wording "VS Code 1.109 experiment-gated, ≥ 1.110 default on", "OSC 52 write VS Code ≥ 1.91 (2024-07-03)", "2026 in tmux ≥ 3.7b", "Windows Terminal Preview 1.25.622.0 (2026-03-05), toggle fixed in 1.25.923.0"; audit every version number taken from can-i-use-terminal against release notes before the matrix freezes | VS Code 1.109.0 shipped `default: false, tags: ['experimental','advanced'], experiment: { mode: 'auto' }`; the default is `true` from tag 1.110.1; can-i-use-terminal's OSC 52 version for VS Code was wrong by two releases; tmux 3.7/3.7a had an end-of-synchronized-update redraw bug fixed in 3.7b | 19 §8, §9, §11, V8, V9, V11, X2, X3, X11, OPEN 7, OPEN 10 |
| A112 | Inside tmux, treat a JevCode-issued query as an Esc-latency cost: tmux holds `ESC` up to a 500 ms floor on `escape-time` while any of its own or a forwarded request is outstanding or a bracketed-paste end marker is split (`tty-keys.c` `if (delay < 500) delay = 500;`, `INPUT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT 500`) — one more reason no handshake or OSC 11 is issued in v1 (A77, A79) | Read from tmux 3.7 source; `tmux` is not installed here so the floor is UNVERIFIED locally (Q21) | 19 §11, V11, X9; 07 §1.2, §1.5, §2.10 |

### 2.12 First-run onboarding, key entry, provider/auth setup and workspace trust (13)

| # | What | Why | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A113 | Non-throwing `config.missingSecrets(mode)` probe run after the first frame (`commandRun` already calls `resolveConfig()` there): returns `['generator.apiKey']`, `['decider.apiKey']`, both or `[]` with `validateGenerator`/`validateDecider`'s emptiness rule; skips the generator key under `--mode jev-only` or `--mock*`; keep `ConfigError` for everything else | `config.generator()`/`config.decider()` throw lazily (`validate.ts:107, :134`) after `render(<App/>)` committed, so a keyless user of a composer would type first and fail second; the first frame stays argv-only (DESIGN §12) | 13 §0.1, §1.1, §5.1, ADOPT |
| A114 | Masked key field = `<Text>` bullets (`'•'.repeat(min(len, columns − 3))`, `*` under `--ascii`) + `useCursor().setCursorPosition` + `usePaste` + the one existing `useInput` routed by wizard step; the buffer lives in a `useRef<string>`, never in React state or the reducer (state holds `{ field, length }` only); no `useFocus` | Ink delivers a bracketed paste as one event on a channel `useInput` never sees; measured 0 key bytes on the pty, no `[Pasted` placeholder, no `<Static>` item in 5 scenarios (bracketed, 40×8, raw chunk, 3 ms typing, split 30 ms); a state snapshot or devtools dump cannot contain the key | 13 §0.4, §3, §4.1, §5.2–5.3, ADOPT; 08 §2.6 |
| A115 | Field input rules: a multi-character `useInput` chunk is a paste; `sanitize` = `sanitizeStream` + strip **all** whitespace (a copied line's trailing newline arrives in the same chunk) + NFC; a paste never submits, Enter does; `MIN_SECRET_LENGTH` (8) is the floor; prefix hints (`sk-ant-`, `sk-or-v1-`) warn but never block; Backspace/Delete drop one code point, Ctrl-U clears, Esc clears or steps back, Ctrl-C prints the fix block and exits 2 | Terminals without bracketed paste deliver the key and its newline as one chunk (scenario C); values shorter than 8 would be ignored by the redactor | 13 §3, §4.1 C, §5.3, ADOPT |

## 3. Consolidated REJECT

| # | What not to do | Why | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R1 | Alternate screen (`\x1b[?1049h`, Ink `alternateScreen: true`) as the default TUI, or an in-app scrolling transcript | Kills native scrollback, search and selection; kitty stack is per screen; Ink `<Static>` becomes pointless; any full-height frame trips the clear predicate; opencode's `--mini` had to invent split-footer/capture-stdout to get scrollback back; Claude Code offers fullscreen only as a flicker workaround; Qwen's alt-screen viewport tears in web terminals (#8659) | 01 REJECT; 02 §2.3, REJECT; 03 §21 R2 (as Gemini's `alternateBuffer`); 04 §13 R2; 05 §4, §16 R1; 06 REJECT; 07 §2.4, §11; 08 §13 R2; 12 REJECT; 15 REJECT (diff viewer) — 9 of the 12 original files; 09, 10, 11 are silent (19 §1) `[corrected per 19 §1]` |
| R2 | Mouse capture (`?1000/1002/1003/1006h`) | Wheel stops scrolling terminal history, drag-select needs a modifier, Ink has no mouse parser; Pi: scrolling "always feels kind of off"; Crush needs a frame cache for wheel floods; Claude Code needed three env vars | 01 REJECT; 02 REJECT; 04 §13 R8; 05 §16 R2; 07 §2.3, §11 — 5 of the 12 original files; the other seven never discuss it (19 §1) `[corrected per 19 §1]` |
| R3 | A custom/native renderer (OpenTUI, SolidJS, Pi's tui, Claude Code's differential renderer, Codex-style DECSTBM inline viewport with raw `ESC[a;br` writes above Ink's frame) | Violates the ink+react rule; Ink 7.1.1 already has sync output, incremental diff, kitty parsing, bracketed paste, cursor control; Codex's inline viewport needed a ratatui fork, per-terminal strategies, a CPR probe and produced #1478/#1685/#1758/#1774 for ~180 B saved per commit | 01 REJECT; 02 REJECT; 04 §3, §11, §13 R1; 05 §16 R3 |
| R4 | Remounting `<Static>` for any toggle (`refreshStatic()` = `clearTerminal` + remount) or editing a rendered `<Static>` row | Multi-second freezes at > 200 items (Qwen #3905); linear replay (293 KB per frame at 1,000 items); Gemini closed the flicker complaint as not planned (#26798); Ink ignores edits to rendered items | 03 §9, §21 R3; 05 §13.3, §14 B, §16 R5; 11 §7 |
| R5 | `useStdout().write` for transcript items instead of `<Static>`; committing items while `suspendTerminal` is active; letting the composer or a pane grow unbounded | Loses Ink's width/wrap and the clear-path replay; items during suspension are silently dropped (measured); anything ≥ `rows` triggers `clearTerminal` + full replay every frame | 08 §5, §13 R7–R9; 11 §7; 06 REJECT |
| R6 | Ink `kittyKeyboard: { mode: 'auto' }` as shipped; kitty flags 2/4/8/16; enabling xterm `modifyOtherKeys` and parsing `CSI 27;m;k~` ourselves outside tmux | Auto writes the query before the first frame, waits a fixed 200 ms with no DA1 sentinel and leaks the reply into `useInput` (measured); release/repeat events double every key; flag 8 turns text into escape codes; Codex gates event types off on iTerm2/Ghostty/tmux because they "leak shortcut release events"; Ink has no branch for the `27;m;k~` form | 07 §1.2, §11; 04 §4, §13 R3; 08 §13 R4, R12; 12 REJECT |
| R7 | Relying on Shift+Enter alone for newlines, or on `key.return` for Ctrl+J, or on `input === '\r'` alone for Shift+Enter | Legacy Shift+Enter is byte-identical to Enter; `\n` parses to name `enter` with no `key.return`; the xterm form injects `[27;2;13~` into the composer | 06 §0 M1, REJECT; 07 §1.4; 08 §13 R3 |
| R8 | Ctrl+K or Ctrl+P as the palette chord; Ctrl+Shift+P, Ctrl+Space, Ctrl+H as distinct bindings; a leader key (`ctrl+x`) as the primary scheme; bare `q` to quit; Ctrl+L as model picker | Ctrl+K = kill-line and Ctrl+P = history in every readline; Ctrl+letter has no shift bit and `\x00`/`\x08` are mis-named/ambiguous in legacy parsing; Ctrl+X is a readline prefix and a 2 s leader timeout adds ambiguity; `q` is a letter in a composer; Ctrl+L = clear-screen elsewhere | 06 §1, §3, REJECT; 05 §16 R4, R13 |

| R10 | `exitOnCtrlC: true`; any `useFocus`/Tab navigation inside the composer; an inverse-video fake cursor; `ink-text-input` / `@inkjs/ui` | Swallows Ctrl-C and misses kitty `CSI 99;5u`; the focus manager eats Tab/Shift+Tab/Esc; a drawn cursor breaks IME/blink/screen readers; both inputs are single-line, string-index, chalk-dependent and stale since May 2024 | 08 §2.6, §7, §13 R1, R5, R6, R10 |
